WHERE [c].[CourtId] = @__court_Id_0 AND [c].[SlotStart] >= @__normalizedWeekStart_1 AND [c].[SlotStart] < @__weekEnd_2 AND [c].[BookingId] IS NOT NULL AND [b].[Id] IS NOT NULL AND [b].[Status] = 2
ORDER BY [c].[SlotStart]
